No escape hatch — no `--skip-findings-audit`, `--allow-todo-without-tracker`, `--ratchet-may-increase`, `--silently-absorb-finding`, `--narrow-vocabulary-to-declare-zero`, `--for-now-in-production` flag.

**§11.4.262 — Machine-created evidence at every gate: every claim of "works" / "passes" / "verified" cites a captured, machine-derived, machine-verifiable evidence artifact produced by the gate — no operator eyeballing, no narrative-only PASS (BACKGROUND :: REMINDER :: IMPORTANT operator mandate, 2026-08-15).** Cascade reference (short form): every claim by the System that something WORKS / PASSES / IS VERIFIED / IS PRODUCTION-READY MUST be backed by MACHINE-CREATED, MACHINE-VERIFIABLE evidence — a file / verdict / measurement produced by the gate itself, cited by path + content-hash + timestamp, with a schema an independent verifier can parse; operator eyeballing, human narrative, "I checked it works", "looks fine", "no error was reported", and every other prediction / guess / assumption about success is FORBIDDEN at the evidence layer (§11.4/§11.4.1/§11.4.6). ALL hold: **(A)** THREE-GATE COVERAGE, each producing machine-created evidence — (1) CONFIRMATION (the change lands correctly: source review, static analysis, format/lint, dependency-clash check per §11.4.110); (2) VALIDATION (the change behaves as designed at each of the §11.4.108 four layers, each with its own verdict + artifact); (3) VERIFICATION (the change satisfies its acceptance contract — §11.4.185 manual QA is the human sufficiency gate, §11.4.236 QA-deploy-readiness the machine one, each with its own captured evidence). **(B)** MACHINE-CREATED — FOUR PROPERTIES: (i) PRODUCED BY THE GATE, not authored by the tester (a screenshot the harness took, not one the operator narrated); (ii) MACHINE-VERIFIABLE (a schema-parseable JSON verdict, JSONL event stream, structured log, an image with an OCR/vision oracle per §11.4.107(11)/§11.4.117, audio with RMS/loudness analysis) an INDEPENDENT verifier can re-check without asking the tester; (iii) CONTENT-ADDRESSED (path + sha256 + timestamp per §11.4.207 so tampering is detectable); (iv) SEMANTIC-CLASS-MATCHED (the evidence class matches the claim layer — a source-grep transcript can never satisfy a "pixel renders green" claim, §11.4.226). **(C)** NO NARRATIVE-ONLY PASS — a PASS whose evidence is a paragraph of natural language authored by an operator or subagent is a violation; the narrative may ACCOMPANY the machine evidence but MUST NEVER substitute for it, and §11.4.185 manual QA's OWN evidence is likewise machine-created (a §11.4.128 session recording, a checklist file with per-item PASS/FAIL + evidence path, a §11.4.238 escape log). **(D)** SELF-VALIDATED EVIDENCE ANALYZERS — every parser that reads an artifact and emits a verdict is golden-good + golden-bad + negative-control validated per §11.4.107(10); an analyzer that PASSes its golden-bad fixture is the bluff and a release blocker. **(E)** FOUR-LAYER BINDING WITH §11.4.108 — each layer's PASS cites evidence PRODUCED AT THAT LAYER (source: static-analysis JSON + review artifact; artifact: build log + fingerprint checksum + a byte-check that the change's bytes landed; runtime: on-target verdict + captured runtime signature + telemetry; user-visible: OCR/vision oracle output or a sink-side probe report per §11.4.13/§11.4.69/§11.4.107); a higher-layer PASS citing only lower-layer evidence is a §11.4.226 wrong-layer violation — the chain runs THROUGH the layers, never SKIPS. **(F)** STANDING DEFAULT (§11.4.126/§11.4.198) from the first prompt, with every dispatch / review / test run / gate emitting its artifact into the §11.4.207 content-addressed evidence store; a status report predicting "should pass" without citing a run's evidence is a bluff at the reporting layer. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): machine evidence does not prove human satisfaction (§11.4.185 remains), and does not license shipping when the evidence disagrees with reality (a missed bug is a §11.4.238 escape triggering a coverage audit and a §11.4.261 vocabulary extension, not a "the machine said green" defence). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-262-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.262`) + recommended gates `CM-MACHINE-EVIDENCE-AT-EVERY-GATE` / `CM-EVIDENCE-ANALYZER-SELF-VALIDATED` / `CM-NO-NARRATIVE-ONLY-PASS` / `CM-EVIDENCE-LAYER-MATCH-108` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.262 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--narrative-pass-OK`, `--operator-eyeball`, `--should-pass-suffices`, `--skip-evidence-analyzer-validation`, `--evidence-optional`, `--pass-without-machine-artifact`, `--predict-success` flag.

**§11.4.263 — Process-group signal-safety mandate: NEVER signal pgid ≤ 1, NEVER trust a mock-derived pid/pgid — validate as int > 1 before every `killpg` / `kill(-pid, sig)` / `pkill` / `killall` call (BOB-126 forensic anchor, 2026-08-19).** Cascade reference (short form): SEVEN forced-logout incidents on the operator's workstation over ~48h were traced by kernel audit trail to a single Python test — an `AsyncMock().pid` left unset returned the `MagicMock.__int__` default of `1`, so `os.getpgid(1) == 1` and `os.killpg(1, SIGKILL)` reached glibc as `kill(-1, SIGKILL)`, SIGKILLing every UID-1000 process (the systemd `user@1000` manager, the desktop shell, tmux, ssh, browsers, the agent session) — with `contextlib.suppress(Exception)` swallowing nothing, because the syscall SUCCEEDED. ALL hold: **(A)** NEVER signal a `pgid ≤ 1` in any language — `killpg(1, sig)` == `kill(-1, sig)` == the disaster syscall on Linux/BSD/macOS. **(B)** VALIDATE `isinstance(pid, int) and pid > 1` AND `isinstance(pgid, int) and pgid > 1` before every process-group signal call (Python `os.killpg`, Go `syscall.Kill(-pgid, sig)`, Rust `nix killpg`, Bash `kill -<pgid>` / `pkill -g`, C `killpg` / `kill(-pid, sig)`). **(C)** TESTS mocking subprocess/proc objects MUST explicitly set `mock.pid = <int>` (never relying on mock-library defaults), MUST patch the killpg call itself, and MUST assert killpg was called with `pgid > 1`. **(D)** DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH four-layer coverage per §11.4.4(b) — a pre-build grep gate, a post-build CodeGraph query, a runtime kernel audit rule (`syscall=kill a0=0xffffffff a1=9`), and a paired §1.1 mutation regression test. **(E)** honest boundary — this hardens process-group signal APIs; it does NOT prohibit legitimate `start_new_session=True` child-owned groups (the guard remains cheap), and it does NOT prevent the kernel OOM-killer or systemd-oomd (§12.6 + §11.4.225 apply there). **(F)** STANDING DEFAULT from the first prompt — every code review scans for killpg calls and verifies the guard, every subagent writing subprocess-cleanup code includes it, and every test-writing subagent sets an explicit `mock.pid`. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.1 / §11.4.4(b) / §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.85 / §11.4.107(10) / §11.4.115 / §11.4.126 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.225 / §11.4.226. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-263-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.263`) + recommended gates `CM-KILLPG-PGID-GUARD` / `CM-TEST-MOCK-PID-EXPLICIT-INT` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.263 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--skip-killpg-guard`, `--mock-pid-optional`, `--killpg-1-is-ok`, `--kill-minus-1-permitted`, `--suppress-catches-syscall`, `--test-may-not-explicit-pid` flag.

**§11.4.264 — Build once, promote ONE immutable content-addressed artifact through every environment; never rebuild per stage (research-derived, 2026-08-20).** Cascade reference (short form): exactly ONE artifact is produced per release candidate and THAT artifact is promoted through every environment; rebuilding per stage silently BREAKS THE CHAIN OF EVIDENCE, because every gate that ran against the staging artifact measured a DIFFERENT binary than the one production runs, making each of those green verdicts a §11.4 PASS-bluff about the shipped artifact — evidence correctly captured against the wrong subject; canon's red-flag form: *"'We rebuild for production to be safe.'"* ALL hold: **(A)** BUILD ONCE per release candidate under §11.4.246 (reproducible + hermetic + SLSA-L2); determinism is what makes a rebuild DETECTABLY equivalent, never a licence to rebuild instead of promote. **(B)** IDENTITY IS A CONTENT ADDRESS, NOT A TAG — a `sha256:…` digest or platform equivalent, never a mutable tag/branch/version string alone ("tags get re-pushed; digests cannot change without changing the digest"); the attack defended is *"the same version, different bytes"*; composes §11.4.111 and §11.4.200 (identity read BACK from the intended target). **(C)** PROMOTION IS A VERIFICATION, NOT A REBUILD — "verify this exact digest is authentic and approved for the next environment", not "make a new thing and hope it's equivalent"; signature, SBOM and provenance TRAVEL WITH the bytes (§11.4.246). **(D)** ENVIRONMENT CONFIG IS INJECTED AT DEPLOY, NEVER BAKED AT BUILD — the artifact is environment-agnostic; baking per-environment config re-creates the per-stage rebuild it forbids, and §11.4.10 still forbids baking a secret. **(E)** ROLLBACK IS DETERMINISTIC BY CONSEQUENCE — point at the previous known-good digest, "not a rebuild-from-source scramble under incident pressure" — the precondition that makes §11.4.265's auto-abort a real undo. **(F)** REBUILD-TO-DODGE IS GATE-EVASION, NOT A RETRY — re-running or rebuilding until a failing scan turns green "launders a real failure into a pass"; retries are counted and surfaced (§11.4.248). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): this guarantees the tested bytes are the shipped bytes; it does NOT prove them correct (§11.4.108/§11.4.40/§11.4.185), does NOT prove the deploy reached the intended target (§11.4.200), and does NOT prove the injected configuration right (§11.4.254) — it closes exactly one link. Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — the consumer supplies its environment ladder, digest scheme and config-injection mechanism as DATA per §11.4.35. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-264-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.264`) + recommended gates `CM-BUILD-ONCE-PROMOTE-DIGEST` / `CM-DEPLOY-CONFIG-INJECTED-NOT-BAKED` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.264 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--rebuild-per-environment`, `--promote-by-tag`, `--bake-env-config-at-build`, `--rebuild-until-green`, `--skip-digest-pin`, `--deterministic-build-means-rebuild-is-safe` flag.

**§11.4.265 — Progressive delivery: traffic-shifted rollout gated by automated analysis on SLO and business metrics, with automatic abort to stable (research-derived, 2026-08-20).** Cascade reference (short form): a release reaches users by a bounded-blast-radius strategy, never an ungated cutover, and progressive delivery is the FIRST gate whose oracle is real user traffic — the deploy-layer instantiation of the §11.4 covenant, catching the defect the suite never encoded at a blast radius of a small traffic fraction. ALL hold: **(A)** the consumer declares its strategy per §11.4.35 from canon's two named forms — CANARY (gradual weighted traffic shift with evaluation pauses; smallest blast radius; old and new run concurrently so schema/compat must tolerate it) or BLUE-GREEN (full parallel stand-up, one-shot cutover, a rollback window; "at the switch, all users hit the new version simultaneously"); for a §11.4.239 critical-invariant surface canon prefers canary paired with blue-green's instant-undo posture. **(B)** THE PROMOTE/ABORT DECISION IS AUTOMATED, NOT A HUMAN WATCHING A DASHBOARD — a controller compares the canary against the stable baseline over successive analysis windows and auto-promotes or auto-aborts and rolls all traffic back to stable, "no human needed"; canon explicitly rejects the manual approval click ("Manual LGTM is a rubber stamp with no receipt; under agents it looks like oversight while providing none"), composing §11.4.240 and §11.4.262. **(C)** THE LOAD-BEARING CLAUSE — THE ANALYSIS MUST INCLUDE BUSINESS METRICS, NOT ONLY INFRASTRUCTURE METRICS; canon names infra-only measurement "the classic mistake": "'HTTP error rate is fine' does not tell you the checkout-completion rate quietly dropped 2% … the failures that matter most are business failures that look technically green", so an infra-only canary is a §11.4 PASS-bluff wearing a rollout's uniform. **(D)** MULTIPLE CONSECUTIVE ANALYSIS WINDOWS, never a single sample (§11.4.50/§11.4.107). **(E)** POST-DEPLOY SMOKE IS A FAIL-CLOSED PRECONDITION OF TRAFFIC (§11.4.252/§11.4.254) — it proves wiring and configuration, and a failed smoke gate aborts the promotion before any user is affected. **(F)** THE DEPLOY GATE IS INDEPENDENT OF THE MERGE GATE — "'got it merged' is not 'got it shipped'". **(G)** THRESHOLDS AND WINDOW COUNTS ARE CONSUMER DATA, NEVER CONSTITUTIONAL LITERALS — canon writes its abort criterion with UNFILLED placeholders ("below X% or p99 exceeds Y ms … thresholds illustrative — set from your SLOs") and warns "A release gate that quotes a stale requirement is itself a bluff". Honest boundary (§11.4.6): progressive delivery bounds blast radius and automates the undo; it does NOT prove the release correct, does NOT replace §11.4.185 manual QA, and does NOT apply where no traffic-shiftable surface exists (an honest §11.4.3 SKIP-with-reason, never a fabricated canary). Canon's progressive delivery is exclusively traffic-shifting — it is silent on feature flags, dark launch, kill switches and shadow traffic, none of which is mandated, and it contains no "a deploy you cannot roll back is not a deploy" claim. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-265-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.265`) + recommended gates `CM-PROGRESSIVE-DELIVERY-STRATEGY-DECLARED` / `CM-CANARY-ANALYSIS-INCLUDES-BUSINESS-METRIC` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.265 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--full-cutover-ok`, `--infra-metrics-suffice`, `--manual-approval-is-the-gate`, `--single-window-analysis`, `--skip-smoke-before-traffic`, `--merge-implies-deploy` flag.

**§11.4.266 — Claim-vs-reality ledger: every ADVERTISED capability is a row, typed from the closed bluff-type vocabulary, and a capability with no passing challenge is a release blocker (research-derived, 2026-08-20).** Cascade reference (short form): canon names this the first and cheapest of its portfolio gates — "every advertised capability is a row with a Bluff type and a severity, and a capability with no passing challenge is a release blocker", making stubbed-core, byte-identical-fork and config-present-but-unwired visible and blocking rather than discovered later. THE DIRECTION OF ENUMERATION IS THE POINT and is what no existing anchor supplies: §11.4.25's coverage ledger, §11.4.52's autonomous-path column and §11.4.169's test-type column are all keyed on FEATURES the project knows it has, so a CLAIM with no feature row is invisible to a feature-keyed ledger by construction — §11.4.266 adds a keying, not a restatement (§11.4.227). ALL hold: **(A)** ONE LEDGER PER REPOSITORY, enumerated from the CLAIM side; the consumer declares per §11.4.35 which surfaces constitute "advertised" (at minimum the README capability set required reachable by §11.4.212, the §11.4.257 manual/guide/FAQ set, the §11.4.254 capability matrix, and the §11.4.259 badge assertions). **(B)** EACH ROW CARRIES A BLUFF TYPE FROM THE CLOSED SEVEN-MEMBER VOCABULARY plus a severity — `green-but-broken` ("passes CI, fails on device"), `coverage-theater` ("the metric lives in prose or a badge, not a failing gate"), `rubber-stamp-verified` ("'verified' is set, never earned"), `stubbed-core` ("the most-engineered code has no caller"), `doc-vs-code-drift` ("a countable claim in prose is wrong"), `config-present-but-unwired` ("the flagship path silently no-ops while the right code exists one dir over"), `byte-identical-fork` ("the name promises X, the code is Y unchanged"); canon states NO severity scale, so the scale is consumer DATA. **(C)** THE TYPE IS A ROUTING KEY TO A COUNTER-GATE — "it turns 'another bug' into 'another instance of type T', which is what lets you install one gate that kills the whole type" — each type binding to the anchor that already owns its counter (§11.4.108/§11.4.262, §1.1/§11.4.224(C), §11.4.146(D3)/§11.4.240/§11.4.249, §11.4.27/§11.4.254, §11.4.106/§11.4.186/§11.4.227, §11.4.124/§11.4.196(F), §11.4.251); §11.4.266 makes the ROUTING mandatory, it does not restate the counters. **(D)** THE RELEASE-BLOCKING RULE — a row whose challenge is absent, never executed, or executed only against a stale artifact fingerprint blocks exactly as a FAILING challenge does (§11.4.135/§11.4.236 absence-blocks-as-FAIL applied to the claim side); this is why the ledger is a gate, not a document. **(E)** THE ESCAPE HALF is §11.4.238's, cited not duplicated. PROVENANCE CORRECTION recorded per §11.4.6: secondary project documentation has called this material an "`FP-1 … FP-14`", "`L-1 … L-16`" or "`F-1 … F-31`" catalogue; measured against the tracked corpus NO `F-n`, `FP-n` or `L-n` identifier exists in any of the nine modules (control-needle proven), so those labels MUST NOT be cited as corpus wording — the real structures are a 14-row numbered failure-pattern table, a 6-bullet portfolio-gate list, and this 7-member bluff-type vocabulary. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the ledger proves every ADVERTISED capability has a passing challenge; it does NOT prove the challenge strong (§11.4.245/§11.4.107(10)/§1.1), the capability bug-free (§11.4.118), or enumerate capabilities nobody advertised — claim-keyed and feature-keyed ledgers are complements, and running one leaves an honestly-stated blind side. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-266-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.266`) + recommended gates `CM-CLAIM-REALITY-LEDGER-COMPLETE` / `CM-UNCHALLENGED-CAPABILITY-BLOCKS-RELEASE` / `CM-LEDGER-ROW-TYPED-FROM-CLOSED-VOCABULARY` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.266 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--ledger-optional`, `--unchallenged-capability-ok`, `--untyped-ledger-row`, `--invent-bluff-type`, `--feature-ledger-suffices`, `--advertise-without-row` flag.

**§11.4.267 — Shared attempt record + converge-on-evidence / escalate-on-stall: a failed approach is never silently retried, and a non-converging loop is itself a signal (research-derived, 2026-08-20).** Cascade reference (short form): canon — "No memory of what was tried → the loop repeats itself. Agents re-attempt discarded approaches. Fix: a shared record of attempts and outcomes (in the task state / PR thread), so a failed approach is not silently retried; and an escalation rule — after N failed iterations, stop and route to a human or a stronger model, rather than burning cycles"; the premise that makes it structural is that "the agent has no memory of prior attempts … it will re-implement the same wrong fix because it does not remember the last three", so the record is externalised memory, not a convenience log. WHAT THIS IS NOT (the §11.4.227 boundary, stated first): §11.4.147 governs a CRASHED agent, §11.4.232(C) a HUNG long-op, §11.4.21 the TERMINAL exhaustion audit — none governs a LIVE, HEALTHY, PROGRESSING loop re-walking a dead end its predecessor already proved dead, and §11.4.267 closes exactly that gap. ALL hold: **(A)** a SHARED, DURABLE ATTEMPT RECORD across every actor that may take the next attempt (a successor agent, a §11.4.147 respawn, a §11.4.176 other track); the §11.4.93 DB and §11.4.116 event stream are the substrates and MUST be reused, not duplicated. **(B)** CONSULTED BEFORE ACTING — the record is READ before the next attempt; a recorded-failed approach is not silently retried, and a re-attempt is permitted only on a recorded material difference (a changed precondition, a corrected §11.4.199 sequence, a new §11.4.115(F) RED); a written-but-never-read record is the §11.4.196(F) configured-≠-in-use failure applied to agent memory. **(C)** CONVERGE ON EVIDENCE — "each loop must monotonically reduce uncertainty", on a SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH ("the verifier's build exit 1 beats the producer's fluent paragraph every time") with CONCRETE FAILURE FEEDBACK (the exact captured error — argv, exit code, failing assertion, offending line — fed back as the next input). **(D)** STALL IS A DETECTED SIGNAL WITH A BOUNDED RETRY AND AN ESCALATION PATH — two named signatures ("the same criterion red after N tries", "oscillating diffs"), the stall itself tracked and trended, and the forbidden shape explicit: "an unbounded retry loop with no ground-truth feedback and no escalation — that is how agents thrash expensively while appearing busy"; escalation composes §11.4.231 (re-tier), §11.4.101, §11.4.66/§11.4.21. **(E)** `N` IS CONSUMER DATA, NEVER A CONSTITUTIONAL LITERAL — canon leaves `N` symbolic in every occurrence and states no numeric, wall-clock, token or cost bound; this anchor mandates that a bound EXISTS and is DECLARED, never a value, and a project enforcing an invented number while attributing it to canon commits the §11.4.6 violation this clause prevents. **(F)** THE LOOP'S DONE-CONDITION is not satisfied while an attempt is in-flight with no outcome, and every escalation reaches a terminal disposition (§11.4.87/§11.4.94/§11.4.97/§11.4.126/§11.4.147/§11.4.197). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the record prevents the RECORDED dead end from being re-walked; it does NOT guarantee convergence (an escalation is an honest outcome), does not prove any attempt competent, and does not substitute for §11.4.102. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-267-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.267`) + recommended gates `CM-ATTEMPT-RECORD-SHARED-AND-CONSULTED` / `CM-STALL-BOUNDED-AND-ESCALATED` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.267 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--no-attempt-record`, `--record-without-reading`, `--unbounded-retry-loop`, `--retry-same-failed-approach`, `--stall-without-escalation`, `--invent-N-and-cite-corpus` flag.

**§11.4.268 — Tamper-evident evidence chain + periodic anchor record: deletion, reordering, and tail-truncation of an accepted evidence record are DETECTED, not merely content-mutation (spec-derived, speckit 002-anti-slop-enforcement, 2026-08-26).** Cascade reference (short form): every accepted evidence record (the §11.4.115(F) machine-written verdict store, the §11.4.116 event stream, or an equivalent evidence-bearing ledger) MUST be tamper-evident against THREE distinct attacks, not only the content-mutation attack §11.4.115(F)/§11.4.226 already cover — (1) deleting an entry, (2) reordering two entries, (3) truncating the tail — and the mechanism detecting each MUST be named, because a forward-hash-chain alone detects (1) and (2) only when the chain is left internally inconsistent and does NOT detect an actor who deletes an entry and then RECOMPUTES the chain forward from that point; closing that gap requires a SEPARATE periodic anchor record the producer can append to but never rewrite. ALL hold: **(A)** every state-changing call WITHIN the consumer's declared COVERED-CALL SET (§11.4.35) produces a matching chain entry; an UNCITED read MUST NOT be required to produce one (guarding against both volume creep and a gate that refuses correct behaviour), while a read result CITED in support of a claim MUST have an entry — the distinction is CITED-vs-UNCITED, never present-vs-absent-in-storage. **(B)** chain verification walks the FULL forward chain, recomputing every link, and detects a deleted or reordered entry left internally inconsistent — single-entry content mutation detection alone does NOT satisfy this. **(C)** THE ANCHOR IS THE ONLY MECHANISM CATCHING A RECOMPUTED-FORWARD DELETION OR A TAIL TRUNCATION — a periodic ANCHOR entry carrying the chain head digest AND the entry count, written on a declared interval to a location the producer can APPEND to but NOT REWRITE ("the count is what makes wholesale store deletion a detected absence rather than silence"), with its strength recorded honestly as `mechanism` (cryptographically or append-only enforced) or `policy` (procedurally enforced only) — `mechanism` MUST NOT be claimed without evidence that rewriting is mechanically prevented, and absent that evidence the recorded strength is `policy`; a verdict of tamper-free MUST name BOTH the chain-walk result AND the anchor cross-check result. **(D)** VERIFICATION THAT CANNOT COMPLETE REFUSES — a chain truncated mid-verification, or an unreachable anchor location, yields an UNVERIFIED status per §11.4.201's conservative-safe default, never a clean pass by default; "could not verify" and "verified clean" are never the same reported state. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): §11.4.268 detects deletion, reordering and tail-truncation of ALREADY-RECORDED entries; it does NOT prove the first entry ever written was truthful (content trustworthiness stays §11.4.115(F) machine-written + §11.4.240 producer≠verifier) and makes the evidence tamper-EVIDENT, not CORRECT; where a project already operates a content-addressed atomically-committed store (§11.4.207), §11.4.268 REUSES that chaining primitive rather than standing up a second divergent implementation (§11.4.227 extend-don't-duplicate applied to mechanism reuse). Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — the consumer supplies its chain implementation, anchor location, anchor interval and covered-call set as DATA per §11.4.35. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-268-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.268`) + recommended gates `CM-EVIDENCE-CHAIN-DELETE-REORDER-DETECTED` / `CM-EVIDENCE-CHAIN-ANCHOR-CATCHES-RECOMPUTE-AND-TRUNCATION` / `CM-EVIDENCE-CHAIN-INCOMPLETE-VERIFICATION-REFUSES` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.268 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--content-mutation-detection-suffices`, `--skip-anchor-record`, `--claim-mechanism-strength-unverified`, `--chain-truncation-passes-by-default`, `--reconstructed-chain-is-clean` flag.

**§11.4.269 — Critic/consensus advisory-only ban AT THE EVIDENCE-ACCEPTANCE SEAM: an ungoverned confidence/consensus signal MAY inform but MUST NEVER substitute for a receipt or adjudicate producer-verifier disagreement; role recorded `advisory`, never entered into an evidence chain — the CONSTITUTION'S MANDATORY INDEPENDENT-REVIEW-VERDICT FAMILY (§11.4.125/.134/.142/.165/.209/.237/.256) IS UNWEAKENED (spec-derived, speckit 002-anti-slop-enforcement, 2026-08-26).** Cascade reference (short form): at the evidence-acceptance / claim-adjudication seam — where a producer's claim of DONE/PASS is accepted or refused and where a producer-verifier DISAGREEMENT is adjudicated — an ungoverned second opinion (a confidence score, an LLM-judge output, or a consensus aggregation over such opinions, none produced under this constitution's own independent-review discipline) is a real, adoptable capability but MUST be admitted in an ADVISORY-ONLY role AT THAT SEAM, never as the mechanism deciding accept/refuse there; the project's own operator decision states it directly — disagreement is adjudicated "on the recorded receipt, never on confidence or argument quality", with an LLM judge explicitly barred from being the gate. ALL hold: **(A)** TWO CLOSED TERM DEFINITIONS — a `critic` is a second/Nth model instance, confidence score or LLM-judge offering an OPINION WITHOUT itself constituting a structurally-separated, receipt-generating review under §11.4.125/§11.4.134/§11.4.142/§11.4.165/§11.4.209/§11.4.237/§11.4.256; `consensus` aggregates multiple such UNGOVERNED opinions; a verdict produced by the mandatory review family is NOT a "critic" for this anchor's purposes. **(B)** a claim whose only support is an ungoverned critic/consensus signal is REFUSED, naming the missing receipt — a recorded receipt (a §11.4.115(F) machine-written verdict, a §11.4.268 evidence-chain entry, or a mandatory review's own recorded GO/NO-GO) remains the only basis for adjudicating disagreement. **(C)** DETERMINISTIC WITH AND WITHOUT THE UNGOVERNED CRITIC — the same claim evaluated once with the ungoverned critic step present and once absent MUST produce the IDENTICAL accept/refuse outcome; a pipeline whose verdict changes when that step is disabled is by definition using it as a gate (this test is scoped to the optional ungoverned critic only — skipping a REQUIRED review is a violation of THOSE anchors). **(D)** ungoverned critic output is recorded with role `advisory` in a SEPARATE store and MUST NOT appear in any §11.4.268/§11.4.115(F) evidence chain; a mandatory review's own GO/NO-GO continues to be recorded as its own anchors require and is not retroactively downgraded. **(E)** an ungoverned critic wired into the acceptance gate collapses ORACLE and GATE into one mechanism NOT independent of the producer's failure surface (correlated model errors, shared blind spots) — the maximum-severity collapse class of §11.4.240/§11.4.249; advisory-only admission avoids re-creating it while retaining the critic's genuine usefulness (surfacing issues earlier, feeding §11.4.102, prioritising human review). **(F)** THE BAN IS SCOPED TO THE EVIDENCE-ACCEPTANCE / CLAIM-ADJUDICATION SEAM — §11.4.125, §11.4.134, §11.4.142, §11.4.165, §11.4.209, §11.4.237 and §11.4.256 each mandate a structurally-separated verifier whose VERDICT genuinely gates, and NONE is relaxed, narrowed, made advisory or made optional by §11.4.269; citing §11.4.269 to skip, weaken or downgrade any of those seven is itself a §11.4.269(F) violation. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the anchor does not forbid using a critic to prioritise human review, surface candidate defects, or inform a human/verifier authoring (not executing) a gate — only its use as an automated GATING mechanism substituting for a receipt-backed oracle/gate/verifier chain. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-269-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.269`) + recommended gate `CM-CRITIC-CONSENSUS-ADVISORY-ONLY` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.269 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--critic-may-gate`, `--consensus-score-is-a-receipt`, `--llm-judge-is-the-gate`, `--critic-output-in-evidence-chain-OK`, `--outcome-may-differ-with-critic`, `--269-overrides-mandatory-review`, `--downgrade-mandatory-review-to-advisory` flag.

**§11.4.270 — Dependency-existence-verdict register: every proposed dependency carries a closed-set existence verdict {VERIFIED, AMBIGUOUS, UNVERIFIED} with citable evidence; adoption is gated behind resolution and behind the wiring-sweep result (spec-derived, speckit 002-anti-slop-enforcement, 2026-08-26).** Cascade reference (short form): a project adopting an external (or internal, cross-module) dependency to close a coverage gap MUST FIRST record for it a closed-set EXISTENCE verdict — VERIFIED, AMBIGUOUS or UNVERIFIED — backed by a citable artifact or an explicit statement that none was found; existence verification is NECESSARY but NOT SUFFICIENT for adoption ("Existing is not the same as working … several verified entries are at initial release versions, one declares a repository that returns 404, and one's 'tamper-evident' claim is on inspection only 'append-only' — which detects nothing if the appender is the party being checked"). ALL hold: **(A)** THE REGISTER — one row per proposed dependency, keyed by a stable identifier (name + version + source per §11.4.111), carrying the verdict and its evidence field. **(B)** VERDICT SEMANTICS, EVIDENCE-BASED, NEVER PROMOTED BY OMISSION — UNVERIFIED is the honest default (a claim with no independent confirmation attempted MUST NOT be silently treated as VERIFIED); AMBIGUOUS is assigned when evidence conflicts or a name collision exists (a dependency name colliding with an unrelated project gets AMBIGUOUS, never VERIFIED), with the specific conflicting evidence recorded rather than a vague "unsure"; VERIFIED requires an independently-reproducible check — a real resolve + checksum/hash match, a real successful call or import, or an equivalent control-needle-proven presence per §11.4.201(7)(b). **(C)** ADOPTION REFUSED while the verdict is UNVERIFIED or AMBIGUOUS; adoption is admitted only after the row resolves to VERIFIED (or the AMBIGUOUS conflict is investigated and re-verdicted). **(D)** ADOPTION IS ALSO GATED behind mapping to one of the project's own recorded UNCOVERED capability gaps (adopting for a capability the project already covers is refused, naming the covering rule — the §11.4.227 duplicate-coverage discipline applied to adoption) AND behind the WIRING-SWEEP RESULT — the adoption step MUST NOT be accepted before the wiring step reports its sweep, so a project never decides "adopt" before learning whether the claimed capability is genuinely reachable and wired (§11.4.124/§11.4.196(F) investigate-before-adopt, the mirror of investigate-before-remove). **(E)** ZERO ADOPTED WITHOUT VERIFIED — the count of dependencies adopted without a VERIFIED verdict is ZERO, checked at the same seam that already refuses unwired-gate names per §11.4.227(A). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the register proves EXISTENCE/RESOLVABILITY was checked and recorded; it does NOT prove the dependency BEHAVES correctly for its adopted purpose (that stays §11.4.244 integration/contract testing) nor that it is SECURE or well-maintained (§11.4.246 supply-chain territory) — it is deliberately narrow, closing the EXISTENCE-CLAIM bluff only. Composes also §11.4.74 (catalogue-first discovery — the VERIFIED-tier check IS that mandated step applied to any dependency), §11.4.31 (an owned-org dependency's `helix-deps.yaml` is itself citable evidence) and §11.4.150 (the deep multi-angle research pass that PRODUCES a row's citable evidence or its explicit none-found statement). Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — the consumer supplies its register location, uncovered-gap set and wiring-sweep mechanism as DATA per §11.4.35. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-270-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.270`) + recommended gate `CM-DEPENDENCY-EXISTENCE-VERDICT-REGISTER` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.270 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--unverified-adoption-OK`, `--ambiguous-treated-as-verified`, `--skip-wiring-sweep-before-adopt`, `--adopt-already-covered-capability`, `--existence-proves-fitness` flag.

**§11.4.271 — Waiver mechanism: the strict FORMALIZATION of allow-with-tracked-debt (unifying §11.4.234(D)'s recorded-deferral, §11.4.236(4)'s explicit-operator-override, and §11.4.248(A)'s deadline-bound quarantine into ONE roster + expiry + tracked-item schema), distinct from refusal (§11.4.21) and closure (§11.4.90) vocabulary (spec-derived, speckit 002-anti-slop-enforcement, 2026-08-26).** Cascade reference (short form): every project needs, on rare occasion, a mechanism to ALLOW a change to proceed despite a normally-blocking missing-evidence condition — never a silent, never a permanent bypass, but a tracked, expiring, human-authorised exception. This is NOT an absent capability being introduced: §11.4.234(D) already permits an explicit, documented deferral flag, §11.4.236(4) already requires an explicit logged operator-set flag for any override of its deploy-readiness seam, and §11.4.248(A) already ships a deadline-bound quarantine for one gate class — three genuine allow-with-known-debt PRECEDENTS this anchor FORMALIZES rather than discovers. What §11.4.271 genuinely adds is the strict, data-bearing, cross-gate SCHEMA those precedents describe only informally and per-mechanism: a ROSTERED NON-PRODUCER authoriser, a MANDATORY UNELAPSED EXPIRY (neither §11.4.234's skip flag nor §11.4.236's override flag currently carries an expiry field at all), and a MANDATORY TRACKED ITEM made mandatory CROSS-GATE. ALL hold: **(A)** the waiver is the ONE formalized schema for an allow-with-known-debt path at a seam that would otherwise refuse; it is invoked explicitly, never inferred, never disables the gate permanently, and a gate already implementing such a path under §11.4.234/§11.4.236/§11.4.248 satisfies §11.4.271 by CONFORMING its existing mechanism to clauses (B)–(D), not by reimplementing one. **(B)** AUTHORISATION MUST RESOLVE TO A ROSTERED IDENTITY, NEVER THE PRODUCER — a free-text authorisation ("looks fine") is refused exactly as an absent one is, a waiver naming a genuinely rostered identity is accepted (a check that refuses EVERY waiver has replaced a bypass with a deadlock), the roster is consumer DATA per §11.4.35, and the producer of the change MUST NOT authorise their own waiver (§11.4.240). **(C)** EXPIRY IS REQUIRED AND MUST NOT HAVE ELAPSED — a waiver with no expiry, or an elapsed one, is refused; an absent expiry is NOT read as "valid forever" (the same downgrade-by-omission the satisfaction path already refuses); an elapsed waiver's gate reverts to blocking automatically, with no grace period and no silent auto-renewal. **(D)** A TRACKED ITEM NAMES THE DEBT — a waiver naming none is refused, "because a waiver is the only ALLOW that leaves the defect unfixed, so it is the one path on which the owed work must stay traceable"; the item is an ordinary §11.4.54-stable-id workable item under the same §11.4.15/§11.4.16/§11.4.33/§11.4.197 lifecycle, and a waiver whose item never resolves is surfaced by the §11.4.261 standing audit. **(E)** COMPOSITION, NOT SUBSTITUTION — a waiver lapsing UNRESOLVED re-enters the §11.4.21 Operator-blocked class for its underlying condition; its tracked item CAN close via §11.4.90 Obsolete if the requirement genuinely changed; neither vocabulary substitutes for the waiver mechanism, and §11.4.234(D) / §11.4.236(4) / §11.4.248(A) each remain the correct narrower mechanism for their own seam class. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the anchor formalizes rather than discovers the category; the schema is deliberately strict because a waiver is the one path permitted to leave a defect genuinely unfixed while still shipping; it weakens no gate for other candidates, and an expiry repeatedly pushed forward without the debt closing is itself a §11.4.261 finding, not a compliant renewal pattern. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-271-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.271`) + recommended gate `CM-WAIVER-ROSTERED-EXPIRY-TRACKED` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.271 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker regardless of context. No escape hatch — no `--free-text-authorisation-OK`, `--waiver-without-expiry`, `--elapsed-waiver-still-valid`, `--waiver-without-tracked-item`, `--producer-may-self-waive`, `--permanent-waiver-renewal` flag.

**§11.4.272 — Dynamic, on-demand skill/extension activation: the active capability surface is the MINIMUM needed now, everything else stays DISCOVERABLE and one command away (User mandate, 2026-09-07).** Cascade reference (short form): every project whose agent platform exposes a capability surface (Agent Skills, plugins, extensions, tool sets) MUST keep the ACTIVE surface at the minimum genuinely needed, with everything else activatable ON DEMAND — never a static all-on set paid for in every turn (measured 2026-09-07 on Claude Code 2.1.263: a 955-skill pool renders as 29,153 B of skill NAMES per turn, or 101,279 B with descriptions — a 3.5x render-mode cliff the project neither controls nor observes; byte counts exact, the ~7,288 / ~25,319 token figures a labelled bytes/4 ESTIMATE per §11.4.6). ALL hold: **(A)** a deliberately small declared `core` always active plus the pool it draws from, both consumer DATA per §11.4.35; anything not `core` is inactive by default, and a `core` entry is paid for in EVERY turn of EVERY session and MUST earn that. **(B)** DISCOVERABILITY IS NEVER TRADED FOR TOKENS — a deactivated capability MUST remain FINDABLE via an always-available index listing the whole catalogue with active/inactive state, and §11.4.102(B) continues to bind unconditionally; saving tokens by making a capability unfindable is FORBIDDEN and is a §11.4 bluff at the capability layer. **(C)** FAIL SAFE, NEVER SILENT (§11.4.201) — a failed activation MUST tell the agent explicitly that the capability is NOT available and exit non-zero; an activation that appears to succeed while the capability is unusable leaves the agent believing it holds a tool it does not, and a dangling or unreadable activation MUST be rolled back. **(D)** CONCURRENCY-SAFE BY REFERENCE COUNTING (§11.4.119/§11.4.176) — where several sessions share one checkout, one session's deactivation MUST NOT strip a capability another is using; claims are reference-counted and released only when no other claimant is PROVEN live (§11.4.180 liveness, §11.4.174 ownership), and a claim whose holder cannot be proven live is reported as such, never silently assumed dead or alive. **(E)** HONEST ASYMMETRY — MEASURE WHAT THE PLATFORM ACTUALLY DOES: measured first-hand, activation HOT-LOADS into an already-running session while deactivation does NOT hot-unload, so the saving is a minimal session-start baseline plus on-demand growth and claiming deactivation frees tokens in the live session is a bluff; equally measured, the platform's own `permissions.deny` `Skill(name)` rule blocks invocation WITHOUT removing the rendered entry and therefore saves ZERO tokens — a first-party mechanism must be TESTED for the property relied on, never assumed from its documentation (§11.4.74 satisfied by evidence). **(F)** A BUDGET GATE bounds the active surface in BOTH the name-only and description-eager renders, so an upstream switch to eager rendering breaks a gate LOUDLY instead of silently costing tokens every turn. **(G)** BOTH MODES — the engine lives in the constitution submodule (§11.4.28/§11.4.80/§11.4.177, by reference, never copied), yet a project NOT vendoring the submodule MUST still get a working command or an HONEST, LOUD refusal — never a silent no-op; resolution order is explicit (environment override, in-project submodule, ancestor's, operator-configured host checkout, documented host fallback, then honest degradation naming WHAT is unavailable, WHY, WHERE it looked and WHAT still works), the degradation using an exit code distinct from "operation failed" and stating that the existing static capability set is untouched. **(H)** PRUNE-TO-CORE IS THE DEFAULT and is NOT a §11.4.122 removal (operator decision 2026-09-07) — session initialisation DEMOTES every non-core capability rather than merely raising `core` (raise-only satisfies (A) in letter and voids it in substance as the surface grows monotonically; measured on the reference project: 19 active / 422 B / ~105 tok-per-turn raise-only versus 3 active / 65 B / ~16 tok-per-turn prune-by-default), and it is not the silent removal §11.4.122 forbids because all three reversibility properties hold BY CONSTRUCTION — LISTED (the always-core catalogue of (B) still shows every pruned capability), ACTIVATABLE (one command returns it, hot-loading per (E)), NOT DELETED (a link-style activation loses only the LINK with its target untouched, and a sole-copy capability is MOVED to a preserved store that is itself a searched source — zero bytes destroyed); an unsafe demotion (another live session's claim per (D), a name collision in the preserved store, a move that fails or lands unreadable, or a capability that would not be re-activatable afterwards) MUST refuse LOUDLY and leave the capability ACTIVE per (C); relocation MUST be relocation-SAFE (path-relative references re-pointed at the identical resolved target before the move, with the post-move readability check as the backstop that refuses and reverts); an EXPLICIT, DOCUMENTED opt-out MUST exist at session scope (an environment override) and project scope (a manifest flag), session scope winning; and where the engine is unreachable, pruning inherits (G)'s honest refusal so a session is NEVER left silently holding fewer capabilities than it believes it has. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): this reduces the per-turn cost of the capability LIST, not of a skill BODY once invoked, is never a licence to hide a capability the task needed, and clause (H) inherits (E)'s measured asymmetry — the saving lands at the next session start for anything already cached, so prune runs AT session initialisation and its in-session effect is never overclaimed. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-272-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.272`) + recommended gates `CM-SKILL-SURFACE-BUDGET` / `CM-SKILL-ACTIVATION-FAIL-SAFE` / `CM-SKILL-PRUNE-PRESERVES-CONTENT` + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code beyond the shipped scripts = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.272 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--all-skills-always-on`, `--skip-activation-engine`, `--hide-inactive-skills`, `--silent-activation-failure`, `--ignore-surface-budget`, `--assume-skill-loaded` flag.

**§11.4.273 — Measuring-instrument verification: a census that INFORMS a decision must be control-needled before its result is believed (research-derived, 2026-09-08).** Forensic anchor: FIFTEEN instances in one session where an ad-hoc measurement returned a confident, plausible, actionable result that was an artefact of the instrument, not a property of the system — a pattern matching only one of two block-opener forms (manufacturing 24 phantom findings); `comm` on numerically-sorted input reporting all 34 items missing; `$?` read after a pipeline turning exit 1 into 0; a field-name typo reading as "every record lost its key binding"; `grep -w` matching `zai` inside `zai-coding-plan` and corrupting a classification; a summary grep assuming `passed, failed` when failure prints `failed, passed`; `while read` dropping an unterminated final line so a one-element list iterated zero times; a mutation applied with the wrong env knob so the REAL tree was tested and the green meant nothing; a guard probe using a command the guard does not target, proving nothing while reading as a negative; a fixture whose two columns held the same string, so a real column-swap mutation survived at 52/0 under a comment asserting that could not happen; a positive control chosen OUTSIDE the instrument's scope reporting a working instrument as broken, and another chosen to BE the question under test so it could not discriminate; an `awk` range whose escaping matched nothing reporting 838 rows where the system held 134, about to gate a DESTRUCTIVE recreate; **a two-literal denylist searched with ONE literal** — both loaded, both lengths printed, one searched — so a live credential was reported eliminated while still handed over in four copy-pasteable lines per file, with the positive control PASSING because it shared the same single needle; and `$?` clobbered by a command substitution in the same argument list, reporting a `pandoc` failure (exit 64) as exit 0. **The gap:** §11.4.201 governs guards/gates (instruments that BLOCK) and §11.4.107(10) governs analyzers (calibrated golden-good/bad); NEITHER covers the ad-hoc census that INFORMS — a grep, a set difference, an exit-code read, a field lookup — whose output is not a verdict but a FACT a decision is then built on, written inline, used once, trusted implicitly, never itself tested. **Mandate:** any measurement whose result will drive a decision, claim, classification or report MUST be control-needled BEFORE it is acted on — **(a)** a POSITIVE control (a value known present must be found: catches too-narrow patterns, wrong field names, wrong paths, wrong knobs, silently-empty stages); **(b)** a NEGATIVE control (a fabricated needle must NOT be found: catches too-broad patterns, accidental substring/word-boundary matches, fabricating defaults); **(c)** an EMPTY result is NEVER a finding alone — absent, could-not-look, looked-in-the-wrong-place and output-shape-differed are four distinct states the raw empty distinguishes between none of (§11.4.6); **(d)** a result CONTRADICTING directly-observed evidence is presumed an instrument fault until proven otherwise; **(e)** the instrument is part of the evidence — a captured claim cites the command that produced it (§11.4.5/§11.4.2). **Honest boundary (§11.4.6):** control needles prove an instrument can tell PRESENT from ABSENT; they do NOT prove it measures the RIGHT property, and a correctly-needled census of the wrong thing is still the wrong answer. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.5/.6/.2/.107(10)/.118/.201/§1.1. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-273-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.273`) + recommended gate `CM-CENSUS-CONTROL-NEEDLED` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.273. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-control-needle`, `--empty-is-absent`, `--trust-the-grep`, `--census-needs-no-proof`, `--obvious-result-exempt` flag.


**§11.4.274 — Mechanical work belongs in a script, not in an agent's context: extract it, test it, and re-scan for it on a cadence (User mandate, 2026-09-08).** Forensic anchor: in one session five review agents consumed ~400–450k tokens EACH, and the dominant cost was not judgment but MECHANICS — copy a tree to scratch, apply a one-line mutation, run a suite, read the summary, restore, verify byte-identical, compare against the previous round, repeat seven times. Every step is deterministic, fixed-input/fixed-output, decision-free: a shell script, executed instead one tool call at a time by a language model at orders of magnitude the cost, with a fresh chance of transcription error at each step (this session logged ten such instrument errors, §11.4.273). **Rule:** work that is DETERMINISTIC, REPEATABLE and contains NO JUDGMENT MUST be delegated to a properly written script or small compiled utility and invoked, not performed turn-by-turn — mutation apply/run/restore/verify loops, suite fan-outs and result tabulation, before/after comparisons, scratch setup and teardown, residue and quiescence scans, census and inventory queries, artifact fingerprinting, evidence collection and layout, and any step already written down as a fixed recipe. **(a) EXTRACT:** a mechanical sequence an agent performs more than twice is a defect in the tooling, not a task — name it, script it, invoke it. **(b) THE TOOL IS GOVERNED:** it ships with actionable documentation (§11.4.18), a user guide/manual entry where operator-facing, and tests validating its behaviour AND producing machine evidence deterministically (§11.4.50/§11.4.5) — including failure paths, exit-code semantics (§11.4.201) and the control needles proving its own measurements (§11.4.273); replacing a slow correct process with a fast unproven one is a regression, not an optimisation. **(c) RE-SCAN ON A CADENCE:** every project periodically scans itself for mechanical work still done by agents (recurring command sequences in transcripts/logs, repeated inline recipes, "run these commands in order" docs) and refactors the findings out — a standing obligation, since new mechanical loops accrete continuously. **(d) HONEST BOUNDARY (§11.4.6) — what MUST NOT be extracted:** JUDGMENT. Root-cause determination (§11.4.102), review verdicts (§11.4.125/§11.4.142/§11.4.194), the decision that a fix is correct, the choice of what to measure, the reading of whether evidence supports a claim, and any assessment requiring "is this the right thing to check?" A script that renders a verdict is a bluff gate (§11.4.201), and automating a review into a green checkmark is the exact PASS-bluff this constitution exists to prevent. The division is: the SCRIPT gathers and executes deterministically and cheaply; the AGENT decides what it means. Cost is NEVER a reason to move a decision into a script. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.18/.50/.5/.141/.201/.273/.124/§1.1. Propagation gate `CM-COVENANT-114-274-PROPAGATION` (literal `11.4.274`) + recommended gates `CM-MECHANICAL-WORK-EXTRACTED` / `CM-EXTRACTED-TOOL-DOCUMENTED-AND-TESTED` + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped). **Canonical authority:** constitution submodule [`Constitution.md`](constitution/Constitution.md) §11.4.274. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no `--skip-extraction`, `--agent-can-do-it`, `--one-off-is-fine`, `--script-the-review`, `--extract-without-tests` flag.
